# Find a pub in London

[Find your local London pub](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/london-pubs?page=7#venue-finder-search)

When it comes to pubs in London, you’re truly spoilt for choice. Whether you’re after historic inns, buzzing bars or somewhere to settle in with your furry friends, there’s a Greene King pub in London that fits the bill.

Our capital is packed with iconic locations, and our pubs are just as full of character – from centuries-old taverns steeped in history to lively spots with riverside views, live music and menus that please the whole family.

Whatever your reason for visiting, we’re here to help you find the perfect pint. Use our pub finder to discover pubs near you in London, book your table online and get ready to explore the best the city has to offer.

## Historic pubs with stories to tell

If old walls could talk, these historic pubs in London would have plenty to say. These legendary spots offer far more than a great pint  – they’re part of London’s story.

![7274_GK_Heritage_YeOldeCockTavern_Holborn_Pub_2023_0013.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/204f97071aa14057a49b8a7f74e95186?v=168be924&t=s445)

## Ye Olde Cock Tavern, Holborn

Almost 500 years old with the narrowest pub frontage in London, [Ye Olde Cock Tavern](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/ye-olde-cock-tavern) is tucked just off Fleet Street. Inside, you’ll find rich mahogany panelling, etched glass partitions and chandeliers that cast a soft glow over traditional interiors.

It’s said to have inspired Charles Dickens, who frequented the area, and Samuel Pepys is known to have sipped ale here. Just a short walk from the Royal Courts of Justice and Somerset House, it’s a fantastic spot to soak up some culture before unwinding with a glass of something tasty.

![7781_GK_HT_George_Southwark_Venue_2024_042.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/94dc7654025a46f98fd84e4d55d3f23c?v=de27f863&t=s445)

## The George, Southwark

The last remaining galleried pub in London, [the George](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/george-southwark), was visited often by Charles Dickens himself. The pub features timber-framed balconies, exposed beams and open courtyards that transport you back in time.

Literary buffs will enjoy knowing Shakespeare’s Globe is just a stroll away, and the Bard’s plays may well have been discussed over ale here. Moments from Borough Market and Southwark Cathedral, it’s a must-visit for history lovers.

![7234_GK_Heritage_ProspectofWhitby_Wapping_Pub_2023_002.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/db01772f2fd24cd8979824d65a162562?v=c675d542&t=s445)

## Prospect of Whitby, Wapping

[The prospect of Whitby](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/prospect-of-whitby) is the oldest riverside tavern in London, with jaw-dropping views over the Thames. This pub dates back to 1520 and has retained much of its original charm, including a pewter-topped bar, flagged stone floors and a no-nonsense wooden staircase.

Notable patrons include Charles Dickens, Judge Jeffries and artists Whistle and Turner. Outside, a riverside terrace gives spectacular views of the Thames and Canary Wharf. Pop in after a walk through London’s [iconic cobbled lanes](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/behind-the-bar/discover-iconic-london-streets-with-greene-king-pubs) or a visit to the nearby Tobacco Dock.

## Riverside pubs with a view

Pints and riverside views? Say no more. These are some of the best pubs with river views in London, perfect for kicking back while the Thames rolls on by.

![The exterior beer garden outside the Anchor, Bankside.](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/2fb4fc17011f467ab3f09c58bb4c148c?v=a76fcc6a&t=s445)

## The Anchor, Bankside

Once the haunt of Shakespearian audiences, [the Anchor](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/anchor-bankside) is a classic pub next to the Globe Theatre. It has lovely rooftop seating, creaky wooden interiors and hidden nooks ideal for cosy conversations.

Outside, the spacious beer garden overlooks the river and fills up fast on sunny days. Inside, expect low ceilings, beautiful wooden beams and nautical touches that nod to its maritime past.

The pub is accessible with step-free entry and accessible toilets, making it a convenient stop for all. With its proximity to the Tate Modern and Borough Market, it’s the perfect pitstop on a cultural day out.

![7976_GK_UC_Salt-Quay_Rotherhithe_Venue_2024_050.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/bcff93acb1d646adbbf7a541c115b5c9?v=3b7ff0f8&t=s445)

## Salt Quay, Rotherhithe

A converted warehouse with panoramic views of the Thames, [Salt Quay](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/salt-quay) features high ceilings, industrial-chic interiors and wonderful, huge windows that flood the space with natural light.

Exposed brick, warm, rustic wood and metal accents lend this pub a modern edge that still feels welcoming. Its generous riverside beer garden is a sun trap in warmer months, and there’s plenty of space for families and dogs. With step-free access throughout and disabled facilities, it’s a welcoming venue for all visitors.

Here, you’re also a short walk from the Brunel Museum and historic Rotherhithe Village, making it an ideal spot to relax after exploring London’s docklands heritage.

![7235_GK_HT_Punch&Judy_Covent Garden_Venue_2024_075.JPG](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/8915855253a14f18bbf0a7ff57a90264?v=6c000063&t=s445)

## Punch & Judy, Covent Garden

Overlooking the vibrant Covent Garden piazza, [Punch and Judy’s](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/punch-and-judy) upper balcony is perfect for people-watching with a crisp drink in hand. Inside, you'll find traditional pub decor with tiled floors, wood-panelled walls and vintage theatre posters paying tribute to the area’s entertainment history.

While it doesn’t sit riverside, its iconic open-air balcony gives a similarly open, summery feel. The pub is centrally located and easily accessible by public transport, with level access on the ground floor and facilities nearby.

The pub shares its name with the famed puppet show that originated right in the square, and it’s just moments from the Royal Opera House and West End theatres.

For more riverside inspiration, have a gander at our guide to [pubs on the Thames](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/behind-the-bar/pubs-on-the-thames) – we’ve got plenty to choose from.

## Live music and late nights

From legendary rock gigs to laid-back acoustic sets, live music pubs in London bring the noise – in the best way.

![4343_GK_UC_Hope-&-Anchor_Islington_Veue_2024_006.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/9abd508f5eda4228a935cce9a11deffb?v=6e1a5037&t=s445)

## Hope & Anchor, Islington

Home to iconic acts like U2 and The Police, the [Hope & Anchor](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/hope-and-anchor) is a cool, North London spot that still hosts live music most nights. The pub’s interiors feature exposed brick, band posters, and a compact stage that gives gigs an intimate feel.

There’s also a jukebox loaded with classics and cult favourites – perfect for music lovers even when there’s no live act on. Upstairs, you’ll find a fringe theatre, and downstairs, an electric basement venue that keeps the party going late.

Located near the bustle of Upper Street, it’s close to the Almeida Theatre and just a short hop from Camden’s music scene. [See what’s on](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/hope-and-anchor/whats-on).

## Dog-friendly hangouts

Exploring London with your four-legged friend? These dog-friendly pubs in London offer water bowls, treats and a warm welcome.

![7171_GK_Heritage_CamdenHead_Islington_Pub_2023_0037.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/cbe0189040af4c45b72ab287cb933192?v=05703e5b&t=s445)

## Camden Head, Islington

Bustling with cheer and character, [Camden Head](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/camden-head) is a lively spot that’s a firm favourite with dog owners thanks to its relaxed feel and pet-friendly touches. Inside, there are colourful interiors, a peaceful beer garden and regular comedy nights.

Nestled snugly in Camden Passage, it’s a great spot to kick back after browsing nearby antique shops or walking along Regent’s Canal.

![7976_GK_UC_Salt-Quay_Rotherhithe_Venue_2023_070.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/fe3d455cb64042f480d62f4776afca28?v=007080cf&t=s445)

## Salt Quay

Yes, again! This riverside pub isn’t just great for the views – Salt Quay is also a brilliant place to bring your pup. There’s plenty of outdoor seating, dog treats behind the bar and spacious interiors that are perfect for larger breeds. A post-walk pint has never looked so good.

Not your style? Sniff out more [dog-friendly pubs](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/dog-friendly) across the city.

## For foodies and families

Fun for all the [family](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/family-friendly). From Sunday Roasts to [crowd-pleasing menus](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/our-food), these family-friendly pubs in London tick all the boxes.

![The interior bar at The New Explorer.](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/b1c96212e43e4f068a8c5bacb50c12f6?v=f0e8bf37&t=s445)

## New Explorer, London

Sleek and modern with a focus on plant-based options and global flavours, the [New Explorer](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/new-explorer) is a decidedly stylish stop in the heart of town. Think plush booths, contemporary art and bold lighting features that add to that debonair city feel.

Just off Regent Street, this is the spot to be for a post-shopping lunch or dinner before catching a West End show.

![7262_GK_Heritage_Tabard_Chiswick_Pub_2023_0029.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/0893b3f2c61c4d129d7cd1e049d6c4ab?v=695598dd&t=s445)

## The Tabard, Chiswick

Whether you’re a meat-lover, veggie, vegan or eating gluten-free, there’s something here to tempt your tastebuds. [The Tabard](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/tabard) is a family-friendly venue that offers a wide-ranging menu that includes all your classic pub favourites.

If you’re feeling a little peckish, there are plenty of lighter bites ,and if you’re searching for London pubs with a [Sunday Roast](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/london-pubs/sunday-roast) – this is the place for you. All served in a relaxed setting that welcomes both locals and visitors alike.

## Matchday magic

From the Premier League to rugby and Wimbledon, sports pubs in London bring the big game atmosphere. Think [live sport](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/live-sport), HD screens showing Sky and TNT Sports, great beer and a buzzing crowd.

![7224_GK_UC_Packhorse&Talbot_Chiswick_Pub_2023_32.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/f6642605c5124f448c2e46fe4133b7e8?v=53742320&t=s445)

## Packhorse & Talbot, Chiswick

A lively local with bags of personality, [the Packhorse & Talbot](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/packhorse-and-talbot) sits proudly on Chiswick High Road, known for its bold green frontage and warm, welcoming feel. Sports fans can catch every moment of the action on Sky Sports and TNT Sports.

There’s paid parking just outside, disabled access and a beer garden that’s perfect for sun-soaked afternoons with friends (furry ones included – they’re dog-friendly too).

## Pubs near London stations

Travelling to or from London? Take a break from the hustle and bustle in one of our pubs near [Kings Cross Station](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/london-pubs/kings-cross-station), [Euston Station](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/london-pubs/euston-station), [Victoria Station](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/london-pubs/victoria-station), [Waterloo Station](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/london-pubs/waterloo-station) or [London Liverpool Street Station](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/london-pubs/liverpool-street-station).

## Explore more London pubs

These are just a few highlights from our incredible line-up of traditional pubs in London, each with its own character and charm. With Greene King, you’re never far from a welcoming local – come and catch the game, tuck into a delicious roast, or just soak up the feeling of the big city.

Check out our [pub food](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/best-pub-food-near-you), discover [pubs near London's squares](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/behind-the-bar/pubs-near-london-squares), and find the perfect Greene King pubs in London for you with our pub finder.

# Find a pub in London

[Find your local London pub](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/london-pubs?page=8#venue-finder-search)

When it comes to pubs in London, you’re truly spoilt for choice. Whether you’re after historic inns, buzzing bars or somewhere to settle in with your furry friends, there’s a Greene King pub in London that fits the bill.

Our capital is packed with iconic locations, and our pubs are just as full of character – from centuries-old taverns steeped in history to lively spots with riverside views, live music and menus that please the whole family.

Whatever your reason for visiting, we’re here to help you find the perfect pint. Use our pub finder to discover pubs near you in London, book your table online and get ready to explore the best the city has to offer.

## Historic pubs with stories to tell

If old walls could talk, these historic pubs in London would have plenty to say. These legendary spots offer far more than a great pint  – they’re part of London’s story.

![7274_GK_Heritage_YeOldeCockTavern_Holborn_Pub_2023_0013.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/204f97071aa14057a49b8a7f74e95186?v=168be924&t=s445)

## Ye Olde Cock Tavern, Holborn

Almost 500 years old with the narrowest pub frontage in London, [Ye Olde Cock Tavern](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/ye-olde-cock-tavern) is tucked just off Fleet Street. Inside, you’ll find rich mahogany panelling, etched glass partitions and chandeliers that cast a soft glow over traditional interiors.

It’s said to have inspired Charles Dickens, who frequented the area, and Samuel Pepys is known to have sipped ale here. Just a short walk from the Royal Courts of Justice and Somerset House, it’s a fantastic spot to soak up some culture before unwinding with a glass of something tasty.

![7781_GK_HT_George_Southwark_Venue_2024_042.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/94dc7654025a46f98fd84e4d55d3f23c?v=de27f863&t=s445)

## The George, Southwark

The last remaining galleried pub in London, [the George](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/george-southwark), was visited often by Charles Dickens himself. The pub features timber-framed balconies, exposed beams and open courtyards that transport you back in time.

Literary buffs will enjoy knowing Shakespeare’s Globe is just a stroll away, and the Bard’s plays may well have been discussed over ale here. Moments from Borough Market and Southwark Cathedral, it’s a must-visit for history lovers.

![7234_GK_Heritage_ProspectofWhitby_Wapping_Pub_2023_002.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/db01772f2fd24cd8979824d65a162562?v=c675d542&t=s445)

## Prospect of Whitby, Wapping

[The prospect of Whitby](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/prospect-of-whitby) is the oldest riverside tavern in London, with jaw-dropping views over the Thames. This pub dates back to 1520 and has retained much of its original charm, including a pewter-topped bar, flagged stone floors and a no-nonsense wooden staircase.

Notable patrons include Charles Dickens, Judge Jeffries and artists Whistle and Turner. Outside, a riverside terrace gives spectacular views of the Thames and Canary Wharf. Pop in after a walk through London’s [iconic cobbled lanes](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/behind-the-bar/discover-iconic-london-streets-with-greene-king-pubs) or a visit to the nearby Tobacco Dock.

## Riverside pubs with a view

Pints and riverside views? Say no more. These are some of the best pubs with river views in London, perfect for kicking back while the Thames rolls on by.

![The exterior beer garden outside the Anchor, Bankside.](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/2fb4fc17011f467ab3f09c58bb4c148c?v=a76fcc6a&t=s445)

## The Anchor, Bankside

Once the haunt of Shakespearian audiences, [the Anchor](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/anchor-bankside) is a classic pub next to the Globe Theatre. It has lovely rooftop seating, creaky wooden interiors and hidden nooks ideal for cosy conversations.

Outside, the spacious beer garden overlooks the river and fills up fast on sunny days. Inside, expect low ceilings, beautiful wooden beams and nautical touches that nod to its maritime past.

The pub is accessible with step-free entry and accessible toilets, making it a convenient stop for all. With its proximity to the Tate Modern and Borough Market, it’s the perfect pitstop on a cultural day out.

![7976_GK_UC_Salt-Quay_Rotherhithe_Venue_2024_050.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/bcff93acb1d646adbbf7a541c115b5c9?v=3b7ff0f8&t=s445)

## Salt Quay, Rotherhithe

A converted warehouse with panoramic views of the Thames, [Salt Quay](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/salt-quay) features high ceilings, industrial-chic interiors and wonderful, huge windows that flood the space with natural light.

Exposed brick, warm, rustic wood and metal accents lend this pub a modern edge that still feels welcoming. Its generous riverside beer garden is a sun trap in warmer months, and there’s plenty of space for families and dogs. With step-free access throughout and disabled facilities, it’s a welcoming venue for all visitors.

Here, you’re also a short walk from the Brunel Museum and historic Rotherhithe Village, making it an ideal spot to relax after exploring London’s docklands heritage.

![7235_GK_HT_Punch&Judy_Covent Garden_Venue_2024_075.JPG](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/8915855253a14f18bbf0a7ff57a90264?v=6c000063&t=s445)

## Punch & Judy, Covent Garden

Overlooking the vibrant Covent Garden piazza, [Punch and Judy’s](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/punch-and-judy) upper balcony is perfect for people-watching with a crisp drink in hand. Inside, you'll find traditional pub decor with tiled floors, wood-panelled walls and vintage theatre posters paying tribute to the area’s entertainment history.

While it doesn’t sit riverside, its iconic open-air balcony gives a similarly open, summery feel. The pub is centrally located and easily accessible by public transport, with level access on the ground floor and facilities nearby.

The pub shares its name with the famed puppet show that originated right in the square, and it’s just moments from the Royal Opera House and West End theatres.

For more riverside inspiration, have a gander at our guide to [pubs on the Thames](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/behind-the-bar/pubs-on-the-thames) – we’ve got plenty to choose from.

## Live music and late nights

From legendary rock gigs to laid-back acoustic sets, live music pubs in London bring the noise – in the best way.

![4343_GK_UC_Hope-&-Anchor_Islington_Veue_2024_006.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/9abd508f5eda4228a935cce9a11deffb?v=6e1a5037&t=s445)

## Hope & Anchor, Islington

Home to iconic acts like U2 and The Police, the [Hope & Anchor](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/hope-and-anchor) is a cool, North London spot that still hosts live music most nights. The pub’s interiors feature exposed brick, band posters, and a compact stage that gives gigs an intimate feel.

There’s also a jukebox loaded with classics and cult favourites – perfect for music lovers even when there’s no live act on. Upstairs, you’ll find a fringe theatre, and downstairs, an electric basement venue that keeps the party going late.

Located near the bustle of Upper Street, it’s close to the Almeida Theatre and just a short hop from Camden’s music scene. [See what’s on](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/hope-and-anchor/whats-on).

## Dog-friendly hangouts

Exploring London with your four-legged friend? These dog-friendly pubs in London offer water bowls, treats and a warm welcome.

![7171_GK_Heritage_CamdenHead_Islington_Pub_2023_0037.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/cbe0189040af4c45b72ab287cb933192?v=05703e5b&t=s445)

## Camden Head, Islington

Bustling with cheer and character, [Camden Head](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/camden-head) is a lively spot that’s a firm favourite with dog owners thanks to its relaxed feel and pet-friendly touches. Inside, there are colourful interiors, a peaceful beer garden and regular comedy nights.

Nestled snugly in Camden Passage, it’s a great spot to kick back after browsing nearby antique shops or walking along Regent’s Canal.

![7976_GK_UC_Salt-Quay_Rotherhithe_Venue_2023_070.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/fe3d455cb64042f480d62f4776afca28?v=007080cf&t=s445)

## Salt Quay

Yes, again! This riverside pub isn’t just great for the views – Salt Quay is also a brilliant place to bring your pup. There’s plenty of outdoor seating, dog treats behind the bar and spacious interiors that are perfect for larger breeds. A post-walk pint has never looked so good.

Not your style? Sniff out more [dog-friendly pubs](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/dog-friendly) across the city.

## For foodies and families

Fun for all the [family](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/family-friendly). From Sunday Roasts to [crowd-pleasing menus](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/our-food), these family-friendly pubs in London tick all the boxes.

![The interior bar at The New Explorer.](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/b1c96212e43e4f068a8c5bacb50c12f6?v=f0e8bf37&t=s445)

## New Explorer, London

Sleek and modern with a focus on plant-based options and global flavours, the [New Explorer](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/new-explorer) is a decidedly stylish stop in the heart of town. Think plush booths, contemporary art and bold lighting features that add to that debonair city feel.

Just off Regent Street, this is the spot to be for a post-shopping lunch or dinner before catching a West End show.

![7262_GK_Heritage_Tabard_Chiswick_Pub_2023_0029.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/0893b3f2c61c4d129d7cd1e049d6c4ab?v=695598dd&t=s445)

## The Tabard, Chiswick

Whether you’re a meat-lover, veggie, vegan or eating gluten-free, there’s something here to tempt your tastebuds. [The Tabard](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/tabard) is a family-friendly venue that offers a wide-ranging menu that includes all your classic pub favourites.

If you’re feeling a little peckish, there are plenty of lighter bites ,and if you’re searching for London pubs with a [Sunday Roast](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/london-pubs/sunday-roast) – this is the place for you. All served in a relaxed setting that welcomes both locals and visitors alike.

## Matchday magic

From the Premier League to rugby and Wimbledon, sports pubs in London bring the big game atmosphere. Think [live sport](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/live-sport), HD screens showing Sky and TNT Sports, great beer and a buzzing crowd.

![7224_GK_UC_Packhorse&Talbot_Chiswick_Pub_2023_32.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/f6642605c5124f448c2e46fe4133b7e8?v=53742320&t=s445)

## Packhorse & Talbot, Chiswick

A lively local with bags of personality, [the Packhorse & Talbot](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/packhorse-and-talbot) sits proudly on Chiswick High Road, known for its bold green frontage and warm, welcoming feel. Sports fans can catch every moment of the action on Sky Sports and TNT Sports.

There’s paid parking just outside, disabled access and a beer garden that’s perfect for sun-soaked afternoons with friends (furry ones included – they’re dog-friendly too).

## Pubs near London stations

Travelling to or from London? Take a break from the hustle and bustle in one of our pubs near [Kings Cross Station](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/london-pubs/kings-cross-station), [Euston Station](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/london-pubs/euston-station), [Victoria Station](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/london-pubs/victoria-station), [Waterloo Station](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/london-pubs/waterloo-station) or [London Liverpool Street Station](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/london-pubs/liverpool-street-station).

## Explore more London pubs

These are just a few highlights from our incredible line-up of traditional pubs in London, each with its own character and charm. With Greene King, you’re never far from a welcoming local – come and catch the game, tuck into a delicious roast, or just soak up the feeling of the big city.

Check out our [pub food](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/best-pub-food-near-you), discover [pubs near London's squares](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/behind-the-bar/pubs-near-london-squares), and find the perfect Greene King pubs in London for you with our pub finder.

# Find a pub in London

[Find your local London pub](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/london-pubs?page=9#venue-finder-search)

When it comes to pubs in London, you’re truly spoilt for choice. Whether you’re after historic inns, buzzing bars or somewhere to settle in with your furry friends, there’s a Greene King pub in London that fits the bill.

Our capital is packed with iconic locations, and our pubs are just as full of character – from centuries-old taverns steeped in history to lively spots with riverside views, live music and menus that please the whole family.

Whatever your reason for visiting, we’re here to help you find the perfect pint. Use our pub finder to discover pubs near you in London, book your table online and get ready to explore the best the city has to offer.

## Historic pubs with stories to tell

If old walls could talk, these historic pubs in London would have plenty to say. These legendary spots offer far more than a great pint  – they’re part of London’s story.

![7274_GK_Heritage_YeOldeCockTavern_Holborn_Pub_2023_0013.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/204f97071aa14057a49b8a7f74e95186?v=168be924&t=s445)

## Ye Olde Cock Tavern, Holborn

Almost 500 years old with the narrowest pub frontage in London, [Ye Olde Cock Tavern](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/ye-olde-cock-tavern) is tucked just off Fleet Street. Inside, you’ll find rich mahogany panelling, etched glass partitions and chandeliers that cast a soft glow over traditional interiors.

It’s said to have inspired Charles Dickens, who frequented the area, and Samuel Pepys is known to have sipped ale here. Just a short walk from the Royal Courts of Justice and Somerset House, it’s a fantastic spot to soak up some culture before unwinding with a glass of something tasty.

![7781_GK_HT_George_Southwark_Venue_2024_042.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/94dc7654025a46f98fd84e4d55d3f23c?v=de27f863&t=s445)

## The George, Southwark

The last remaining galleried pub in London, [the George](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/george-southwark), was visited often by Charles Dickens himself. The pub features timber-framed balconies, exposed beams and open courtyards that transport you back in time.

Literary buffs will enjoy knowing Shakespeare’s Globe is just a stroll away, and the Bard’s plays may well have been discussed over ale here. Moments from Borough Market and Southwark Cathedral, it’s a must-visit for history lovers.

![7234_GK_Heritage_ProspectofWhitby_Wapping_Pub_2023_002.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/db01772f2fd24cd8979824d65a162562?v=c675d542&t=s445)

## Prospect of Whitby, Wapping

[The prospect of Whitby](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/prospect-of-whitby) is the oldest riverside tavern in London, with jaw-dropping views over the Thames. This pub dates back to 1520 and has retained much of its original charm, including a pewter-topped bar, flagged stone floors and a no-nonsense wooden staircase.

Notable patrons include Charles Dickens, Judge Jeffries and artists Whistle and Turner. Outside, a riverside terrace gives spectacular views of the Thames and Canary Wharf. Pop in after a walk through London’s [iconic cobbled lanes](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/behind-the-bar/discover-iconic-london-streets-with-greene-king-pubs) or a visit to the nearby Tobacco Dock.

## Riverside pubs with a view

Pints and riverside views? Say no more. These are some of the best pubs with river views in London, perfect for kicking back while the Thames rolls on by.

![The exterior beer garden outside the Anchor, Bankside.](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/2fb4fc17011f467ab3f09c58bb4c148c?v=a76fcc6a&t=s445)

## The Anchor, Bankside

Once the haunt of Shakespearian audiences, [the Anchor](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/anchor-bankside) is a classic pub next to the Globe Theatre. It has lovely rooftop seating, creaky wooden interiors and hidden nooks ideal for cosy conversations.

Outside, the spacious beer garden overlooks the river and fills up fast on sunny days. Inside, expect low ceilings, beautiful wooden beams and nautical touches that nod to its maritime past.

The pub is accessible with step-free entry and accessible toilets, making it a convenient stop for all. With its proximity to the Tate Modern and Borough Market, it’s the perfect pitstop on a cultural day out.

![7976_GK_UC_Salt-Quay_Rotherhithe_Venue_2024_050.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/bcff93acb1d646adbbf7a541c115b5c9?v=3b7ff0f8&t=s445)

## Salt Quay, Rotherhithe

A converted warehouse with panoramic views of the Thames, [Salt Quay](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/salt-quay) features high ceilings, industrial-chic interiors and wonderful, huge windows that flood the space with natural light.

Exposed brick, warm, rustic wood and metal accents lend this pub a modern edge that still feels welcoming. Its generous riverside beer garden is a sun trap in warmer months, and there’s plenty of space for families and dogs. With step-free access throughout and disabled facilities, it’s a welcoming venue for all visitors.

Here, you’re also a short walk from the Brunel Museum and historic Rotherhithe Village, making it an ideal spot to relax after exploring London’s docklands heritage.

![7235_GK_HT_Punch&Judy_Covent Garden_Venue_2024_075.JPG](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/8915855253a14f18bbf0a7ff57a90264?v=6c000063&t=s445)

## Punch & Judy, Covent Garden

Overlooking the vibrant Covent Garden piazza, [Punch and Judy’s](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/punch-and-judy) upper balcony is perfect for people-watching with a crisp drink in hand. Inside, you'll find traditional pub decor with tiled floors, wood-panelled walls and vintage theatre posters paying tribute to the area’s entertainment history.

While it doesn’t sit riverside, its iconic open-air balcony gives a similarly open, summery feel. The pub is centrally located and easily accessible by public transport, with level access on the ground floor and facilities nearby.

The pub shares its name with the famed puppet show that originated right in the square, and it’s just moments from the Royal Opera House and West End theatres.

For more riverside inspiration, have a gander at our guide to [pubs on the Thames](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/behind-the-bar/pubs-on-the-thames) – we’ve got plenty to choose from.

## Live music and late nights

From legendary rock gigs to laid-back acoustic sets, live music pubs in London bring the noise – in the best way.

![4343_GK_UC_Hope-&-Anchor_Islington_Veue_2024_006.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/9abd508f5eda4228a935cce9a11deffb?v=6e1a5037&t=s445)

## Hope & Anchor, Islington

Home to iconic acts like U2 and The Police, the [Hope & Anchor](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/hope-and-anchor) is a cool, North London spot that still hosts live music most nights. The pub’s interiors feature exposed brick, band posters, and a compact stage that gives gigs an intimate feel.

There’s also a jukebox loaded with classics and cult favourites – perfect for music lovers even when there’s no live act on. Upstairs, you’ll find a fringe theatre, and downstairs, an electric basement venue that keeps the party going late.

Located near the bustle of Upper Street, it’s close to the Almeida Theatre and just a short hop from Camden’s music scene. [See what’s on](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/hope-and-anchor/whats-on).

## Dog-friendly hangouts

Exploring London with your four-legged friend? These dog-friendly pubs in London offer water bowls, treats and a warm welcome.

![7171_GK_Heritage_CamdenHead_Islington_Pub_2023_0037.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/cbe0189040af4c45b72ab287cb933192?v=05703e5b&t=s445)

## Camden Head, Islington

Bustling with cheer and character, [Camden Head](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/camden-head) is a lively spot that’s a firm favourite with dog owners thanks to its relaxed feel and pet-friendly touches. Inside, there are colourful interiors, a peaceful beer garden and regular comedy nights.

Nestled snugly in Camden Passage, it’s a great spot to kick back after browsing nearby antique shops or walking along Regent’s Canal.

![7976_GK_UC_Salt-Quay_Rotherhithe_Venue_2023_070.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/fe3d455cb64042f480d62f4776afca28?v=007080cf&t=s445)

## Salt Quay

Yes, again! This riverside pub isn’t just great for the views – Salt Quay is also a brilliant place to bring your pup. There’s plenty of outdoor seating, dog treats behind the bar and spacious interiors that are perfect for larger breeds. A post-walk pint has never looked so good.

Not your style? Sniff out more [dog-friendly pubs](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/dog-friendly) across the city.

## For foodies and families

Fun for all the [family](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/family-friendly). From Sunday Roasts to [crowd-pleasing menus](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/our-food), these family-friendly pubs in London tick all the boxes.

![The interior bar at The New Explorer.](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/b1c96212e43e4f068a8c5bacb50c12f6?v=f0e8bf37&t=s445)

## New Explorer, London

Sleek and modern with a focus on plant-based options and global flavours, the [New Explorer](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/new-explorer) is a decidedly stylish stop in the heart of town. Think plush booths, contemporary art and bold lighting features that add to that debonair city feel.

Just off Regent Street, this is the spot to be for a post-shopping lunch or dinner before catching a West End show.

![7262_GK_Heritage_Tabard_Chiswick_Pub_2023_0029.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/0893b3f2c61c4d129d7cd1e049d6c4ab?v=695598dd&t=s445)

## The Tabard, Chiswick

Whether you’re a meat-lover, veggie, vegan or eating gluten-free, there’s something here to tempt your tastebuds. [The Tabard](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/tabard) is a family-friendly venue that offers a wide-ranging menu that includes all your classic pub favourites.

If you’re feeling a little peckish, there are plenty of lighter bites ,and if you’re searching for London pubs with a [Sunday Roast](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/london-pubs/sunday-roast) – this is the place for you. All served in a relaxed setting that welcomes both locals and visitors alike.

## Matchday magic

From the Premier League to rugby and Wimbledon, sports pubs in London bring the big game atmosphere. Think [live sport](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/live-sport), HD screens showing Sky and TNT Sports, great beer and a buzzing crowd.

![7224_GK_UC_Packhorse&Talbot_Chiswick_Pub_2023_32.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/f6642605c5124f448c2e46fe4133b7e8?v=53742320&t=s445)

## Packhorse & Talbot, Chiswick

A lively local with bags of personality, [the Packhorse & Talbot](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/packhorse-and-talbot) sits proudly on Chiswick High Road, known for its bold green frontage and warm, welcoming feel. Sports fans can catch every moment of the action on Sky Sports and TNT Sports.

There’s paid parking just outside, disabled access and a beer garden that’s perfect for sun-soaked afternoons with friends (furry ones included – they’re dog-friendly too).

## Pubs near London stations

Travelling to or from London? Take a break from the hustle and bustle in one of our pubs near [Kings Cross Station](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/london-pubs/kings-cross-station), [Euston Station](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/london-pubs/euston-station), [Victoria Station](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/london-pubs/victoria-station), [Waterloo Station](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/london-pubs/waterloo-station) or [London Liverpool Street Station](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/london-pubs/liverpool-street-station).

These are just a few highlights from our incredible line-up of traditional pubs in London, each with its own character and charm. With Greene King, you’re never far from a welcoming local – come and catch the game, tuck into a delicious roast, or just soak up the feeling of the big city.

# Find a pub in London

[Find your local London pub](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/london-pubs?page=10#venue-finder-search)

When it comes to pubs in London, you’re truly spoilt for choice. Whether you’re after historic inns, buzzing bars or somewhere to settle in with your furry friends, there’s a Greene King pub in London that fits the bill.

Our capital is packed with iconic locations, and our pubs are just as full of character – from centuries-old taverns steeped in history to lively spots with riverside views, live music and menus that please the whole family.

Whatever your reason for visiting, we’re here to help you find the perfect pint. Use our pub finder to discover pubs near you in London, book your table online and get ready to explore the best the city has to offer.

## Historic pubs with stories to tell

If old walls could talk, these historic pubs in London would have plenty to say. These legendary spots offer far more than a great pint  – they’re part of London’s story.

![7274_GK_Heritage_YeOldeCockTavern_Holborn_Pub_2023_0013.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/204f97071aa14057a49b8a7f74e95186?v=168be924&t=s445)

## Ye Olde Cock Tavern, Holborn

Almost 500 years old with the narrowest pub frontage in London, [Ye Olde Cock Tavern](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/ye-olde-cock-tavern) is tucked just off Fleet Street. Inside, you’ll find rich mahogany panelling, etched glass partitions and chandeliers that cast a soft glow over traditional interiors.

It’s said to have inspired Charles Dickens, who frequented the area, and Samuel Pepys is known to have sipped ale here. Just a short walk from the Royal Courts of Justice and Somerset House, it’s a fantastic spot to soak up some culture before unwinding with a glass of something tasty.

![7781_GK_HT_George_Southwark_Venue_2024_042.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/94dc7654025a46f98fd84e4d55d3f23c?v=de27f863&t=s445)

## The George, Southwark

The last remaining galleried pub in London, [the George](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/george-southwark), was visited often by Charles Dickens himself. The pub features timber-framed balconies, exposed beams and open courtyards that transport you back in time.

Literary buffs will enjoy knowing Shakespeare’s Globe is just a stroll away, and the Bard’s plays may well have been discussed over ale here. Moments from Borough Market and Southwark Cathedral, it’s a must-visit for history lovers.

![7234_GK_Heritage_ProspectofWhitby_Wapping_Pub_2023_002.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/db01772f2fd24cd8979824d65a162562?v=c675d542&t=s445)

## Prospect of Whitby, Wapping

[The prospect of Whitby](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/prospect-of-whitby) is the oldest riverside tavern in London, with jaw-dropping views over the Thames. This pub dates back to 1520 and has retained much of its original charm, including a pewter-topped bar, flagged stone floors and a no-nonsense wooden staircase.

Notable patrons include Charles Dickens, Judge Jeffries and artists Whistle and Turner. Outside, a riverside terrace gives spectacular views of the Thames and Canary Wharf. Pop in after a walk through London’s [iconic cobbled lanes](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/behind-the-bar/discover-iconic-london-streets-with-greene-king-pubs) or a visit to the nearby Tobacco Dock.

## Riverside pubs with a view

Pints and riverside views? Say no more. These are some of the best pubs with river views in London, perfect for kicking back while the Thames rolls on by.

![The exterior beer garden outside the Anchor, Bankside.](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/2fb4fc17011f467ab3f09c58bb4c148c?v=a76fcc6a&t=s445)

## The Anchor, Bankside

Once the haunt of Shakespearian audiences, [the Anchor](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/anchor-bankside) is a classic pub next to the Globe Theatre. It has lovely rooftop seating, creaky wooden interiors and hidden nooks ideal for cosy conversations.

Outside, the spacious beer garden overlooks the river and fills up fast on sunny days. Inside, expect low ceilings, beautiful wooden beams and nautical touches that nod to its maritime past.

The pub is accessible with step-free entry and accessible toilets, making it a convenient stop for all. With its proximity to the Tate Modern and Borough Market, it’s the perfect pitstop on a cultural day out.

![7976_GK_UC_Salt-Quay_Rotherhithe_Venue_2024_050.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/bcff93acb1d646adbbf7a541c115b5c9?v=3b7ff0f8&t=s445)

## Salt Quay, Rotherhithe

A converted warehouse with panoramic views of the Thames, [Salt Quay](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/salt-quay) features high ceilings, industrial-chic interiors and wonderful, huge windows that flood the space with natural light.

Exposed brick, warm, rustic wood and metal accents lend this pub a modern edge that still feels welcoming. Its generous riverside beer garden is a sun trap in warmer months, and there’s plenty of space for families and dogs. With step-free access throughout and disabled facilities, it’s a welcoming venue for all visitors.

Here, you’re also a short walk from the Brunel Museum and historic Rotherhithe Village, making it an ideal spot to relax after exploring London’s docklands heritage.

![7235_GK_HT_Punch&Judy_Covent Garden_Venue_2024_075.JPG](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/8915855253a14f18bbf0a7ff57a90264?v=6c000063&t=s445)

## Punch & Judy, Covent Garden

Overlooking the vibrant Covent Garden piazza, [Punch and Judy’s](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/punch-and-judy) upper balcony is perfect for people-watching with a crisp drink in hand. Inside, you'll find traditional pub decor with tiled floors, wood-panelled walls and vintage theatre posters paying tribute to the area’s entertainment history.

While it doesn’t sit riverside, its iconic open-air balcony gives a similarly open, summery feel. The pub is centrally located and easily accessible by public transport, with level access on the ground floor and facilities nearby.

The pub shares its name with the famed puppet show that originated right in the square, and it’s just moments from the Royal Opera House and West End theatres.

For more riverside inspiration, have a gander at our guide to [pubs on the Thames](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/behind-the-bar/pubs-on-the-thames) – we’ve got plenty to choose from.

## Live music and late nights

From legendary rock gigs to laid-back acoustic sets, live music pubs in London bring the noise – in the best way.

![4343_GK_UC_Hope-&-Anchor_Islington_Veue_2024_006.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/9abd508f5eda4228a935cce9a11deffb?v=6e1a5037&t=s445)

## Hope & Anchor, Islington

Home to iconic acts like U2 and The Police, the [Hope & Anchor](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/hope-and-anchor) is a cool, North London spot that still hosts live music most nights. The pub’s interiors feature exposed brick, band posters, and a compact stage that gives gigs an intimate feel.

There’s also a jukebox loaded with classics and cult favourites – perfect for music lovers even when there’s no live act on. Upstairs, you’ll find a fringe theatre, and downstairs, an electric basement venue that keeps the party going late.

Located near the bustle of Upper Street, it’s close to the Almeida Theatre and just a short hop from Camden’s music scene. [See what’s on](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/hope-and-anchor/whats-on).

## Dog-friendly hangouts

Exploring London with your four-legged friend? These dog-friendly pubs in London offer water bowls, treats and a warm welcome.

![7171_GK_Heritage_CamdenHead_Islington_Pub_2023_0037.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/cbe0189040af4c45b72ab287cb933192?v=05703e5b&t=s445)

## Camden Head, Islington

Bustling with cheer and character, [Camden Head](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/camden-head) is a lively spot that’s a firm favourite with dog owners thanks to its relaxed feel and pet-friendly touches. Inside, there are colourful interiors, a peaceful beer garden and regular comedy nights.

Nestled snugly in Camden Passage, it’s a great spot to kick back after browsing nearby antique shops or walking along Regent’s Canal.

![7976_GK_UC_Salt-Quay_Rotherhithe_Venue_2023_070.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/fe3d455cb64042f480d62f4776afca28?v=007080cf&t=s445)

## Salt Quay

Yes, again! This riverside pub isn’t just great for the views – Salt Quay is also a brilliant place to bring your pup. There’s plenty of outdoor seating, dog treats behind the bar and spacious interiors that are perfect for larger breeds. A post-walk pint has never looked so good.

Not your style? Sniff out more [dog-friendly pubs](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/dog-friendly) across the city.

## For foodies and families

Fun for all the [family](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/family-friendly). From Sunday Roasts to [crowd-pleasing menus](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/our-food), these family-friendly pubs in London tick all the boxes.

![The interior bar at The New Explorer.](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/b1c96212e43e4f068a8c5bacb50c12f6?v=f0e8bf37&t=s445)

## New Explorer, London

Sleek and modern with a focus on plant-based options and global flavours, the [New Explorer](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/new-explorer) is a decidedly stylish stop in the heart of town. Think plush booths, contemporary art and bold lighting features that add to that debonair city feel.

Just off Regent Street, this is the spot to be for a post-shopping lunch or dinner before catching a West End show.

![7262_GK_Heritage_Tabard_Chiswick_Pub_2023_0029.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/0893b3f2c61c4d129d7cd1e049d6c4ab?v=695598dd&t=s445)

## The Tabard, Chiswick

Whether you’re a meat-lover, veggie, vegan or eating gluten-free, there’s something here to tempt your tastebuds. [The Tabard](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/tabard) is a family-friendly venue that offers a wide-ranging menu that includes all your classic pub favourites.

If you’re feeling a little peckish, there are plenty of lighter bites ,and if you’re searching for London pubs with a [Sunday Roast](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/london-pubs/sunday-roast) – this is the place for you. All served in a relaxed setting that welcomes both locals and visitors alike.

## Matchday magic

From the Premier League to rugby and Wimbledon, sports pubs in London bring the big game atmosphere. Think [live sport](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/live-sport), HD screens showing Sky and TNT Sports, great beer and a buzzing crowd.

![7224_GK_UC_Packhorse&Talbot_Chiswick_Pub_2023_32.jpg](https://gkbr-p-001.sitecorecontenthub.cloud/api/public/content/f6642605c5124f448c2e46fe4133b7e8?v=53742320&t=s445)

## Packhorse & Talbot, Chiswick

A lively local with bags of personality, [the Packhorse & Talbot](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs/greater-london/packhorse-and-talbot) sits proudly on Chiswick High Road, known for its bold green frontage and warm, welcoming feel. Sports fans can catch every moment of the action on Sky Sports and TNT Sports.

There’s paid parking just outside, disabled access and a beer garden that’s perfect for sun-soaked afternoons with friends (furry ones included – they’re dog-friendly too).

## Pubs near London stations

Travelling to or from London? Take a break from the hustle and bustle in one of our pubs near [Kings Cross Station](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/london-pubs/kings-cross-station), [Euston Station](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/london-pubs/euston-station), [Victoria Station](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/london-pubs/victoria-station), [Waterloo Station](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/london-pubs/waterloo-station) or [London Liverpool Street Station](https://www.greeneking.co.uk/pubs-near-me/london-pubs/liverpool-street-station).

## Explore more London pubs

These are just a few highlights from our incredible line-up of traditional pubs in London, each with its own character and charm. With Greene King, you’re never far from a welcoming local – come and catch the game, tuck into a delicious roast, or just soak up the feeling of the big city.
